transition-transform duration-200" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M10 4v12M4 10h12"></path></svg></button></h3><div id="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-0-panel" role="region" aria-labelledby="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-0-button" class="overflow-hidden" inert="" style="height:0px;opacity:0"><div class="text-primary max-w-2xl pr-11 pb-6 text-base leading-7 [&amp;_p+p]:mt-3 [&amp;_a]:text-primary/80 [&amp;_a]:underline [&amp;_a]:underline-offset-2 [&amp;_a:hover]:text-primary"><p>We recommend Premium for new AI users, Super for daily agent users, and Ultra for power users. Plus is recommended for social creators with limited AI usage.</p><p>The Teams plan is recommended for professionals collaborating with others, and larger organizations that need invoicing, pooled usage, or advanced security should choose Enterprise.</p></div></div></div><div><h3 class="m-0 text-base font-normal"><button id="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-1-button" type="button" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-1-panel" class="focus-visible:ring-primary/30 flex w-full items-start justify-between gap-6 py-5 text-left focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-2"><span class="text-primary text-base font-medium leading-6">What are my payment options?</span><svg aria-hidden="true" class="text-primary/60 mt-0.5 size-5 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M10 4v12M4 10h12"></path></svg></button></h3><div id="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-1-panel" role="region" aria-labelledby="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-1-button" class="overflow-hidden" inert="" style="height:0px;opacity:0"><div class="text-primary max-w-2xl pr-11 pb-6 text-base leading-7 [&amp;_p+p]:mt-3 [&amp;_a]:text-primary/80 [&amp;_a]:underline [&amp;_a]:underline-offset-2 [&amp;_a:hover]:text-primary"><p>Self-serve plans support all major credit and debit cards. For invoice-based billing and wire transfers, please<!-- --> <a data-track="pricing.faq.contact-sales" href="/contact-sales">contact us</a> <!-- -->to discuss the Enterprise plan.</p></div></div></div><div><h3 class="m-0 text-base font-normal"><button id="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-2-button" type="button" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-2-panel" class="focus-visible:ring-primary/30 flex w-full items-start justify-between gap-6 py-5 text-left focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-2"><span class="text-primary text-base font-medium leading-6">How do usage limits work?</span><svg aria-hidden="true" class="text-primary/60 mt-0.5 size-5 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M10 4v12M4 10h12"></path></svg></button></h3><div id="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-2-panel" role="region" aria-labelledby="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-2-button" class="overflow-hidden" inert="" style="height:0px;opacity:0"><div class="text-primary max-w-2xl pr-11 pb-6 text-base leading-7 [&amp;_p+p]:mt-3 [&amp;_a]:text-primary/80 [&amp;_a]:underline [&amp;_a]:underline-offset-2 [&amp;_a:hover]:text-primary"><p>Every tier includes a pool of AI usage shared across Grok and Cursor. Within Grok, the pool covers Chat, Imagine, Voice, and Bot. Higher tiers include larger pools, and usage costs vary by product. Your allowance refreshes weekly, so one heavy day never wipes out your whole month.</p><p>If you reach your included usage, you can keep going with top-ups, or wait for your allowance to refresh. You’ll always see your remaining usage in settings. See<!-- --> <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://cursor.com/help/models-and-usage/usage-limits">our docs</a> <!-- -->for more details.</p></div></div></div><div><h3 class="m-0 text-base font-normal"><button id="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-3-button" type="button" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-3-panel" class="focus-visible:ring-primary/30 flex w-full items-start justify-between gap-6 py-5 text-left focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-2"><span class="text-primary text-base font-medium leading-6">Is the Grok API included in my plan?</span><svg aria-hidden="true" class="text-primary/60 mt-0.5 size-5 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M10 4v12M4 10h12"></path></svg></button></h3><div id="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-3-panel" role="region" aria-labelledby="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-3-button" class="overflow-hidden" inert="" style="height:0px;opacity:0"><div class="text-primary max-w-2xl pr-11 pb-6 text-base leading-7 [&amp;_p+p]:mt-3 [&amp;_a]:text-primary/80 [&amp;_a]:underline [&amp;_a]:underline-offset-2 [&amp;_a:hover]:text-primary"><p>Paid plans include Grok API access. API usage is billed separately at standard model rates and doesn’t draw from your plan’s usage pool. See<!-- --> <a target="_blank" data-track="pricing.faq.model-pricing" href="https://docs.x.ai/developers/pricing">model pricing</a>.</p></div></div></div><div><h3 class="m-0 text-base font-normal"><button id="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-4-button" type="button" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-4-panel" class="focus-visible:ring-primary/30 flex w-full items-start justify-between gap-6 py-5 text-left focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-2"><span class="text-primary text-base font-medium leading-6">What models are supported?</span><svg aria-hidden="true" class="text-primary/60 mt-0.5 size-5 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M10 4v12M4 10h12"></path></svg></button></h3><div id="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-4-panel" role="region" aria-labelledby="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-4-button" class="overflow-hidden" inert="" style="height:0px;opacity:0"><div class="text-primary max-w-2xl pr-11 pb-6 text-base leading-7 [&amp;_p+p]:mt-3 [&amp;_a]:text-primary/80 [&amp;_a]:underline [&amp;_a]:underline-offset-2 [&amp;_a:hover]:text-primary"><p>The self-serve plans include generous usage of Grok models but do not include an allowance for third-party models. Third-party models remain available in Cursor at API list prices. You pay for exactly what you use, and you can set spending caps to manage costs.</p></div></div></div><div><h3 class="m-0 text-base font-normal"><button id="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-5-button" type="button" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-5-panel" class="focus-visible:ring-primary/30 flex w-full items-start justify-between gap-6 py-5 text-left focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-2"><span class="text-primary text-base font-medium leading-6">Are prices inclusive of taxes?</span><svg aria-hidden="true" class="text-primary/60 mt-0.5 size-5 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M10 4v12M4 10h12"></path></svg></button></h3><div id="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-5-panel" role="region" aria-labelledby="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-5-button" class="overflow-hidden" inert="" style="height:0px;opacity:0"><div class="text-primary max-w-2xl pr-11 pb-6 text-base leading-7 [&amp;_p+p]:mt-3 [&amp;_a]:text-primary/80 [&amp;_a]:underline [&amp;_a]:underline-offset-2 [&amp;_a:hover]:text-primary"><p>All prices are exclusive of any applicable taxes. See our<!-- --> <a href="/legal/bundled-subscription-terms">Subscription Terms</a> for more information.</p></div></div></div><div><h3 class="m-0 text-base font-normal"><button id="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-6-button" type="button" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-6-panel" class="focus-visible:ring-primary/30 flex w-full items-start justify-between gap-6 py-5 text-left focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-2"><span class="text-primary text-base font-medium leading-6">Can I purchase from a reseller or third party?</span><svg aria-hidden="true" class="text-primary/60 mt-0.5 size-5 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M10 4v12M4 10h12"></path></svg></button></h3><div id="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-6-panel" role="region" aria-labelledby="_R_2lbsnpfiv9falb_-6-button" class="overflow-hidden" inert="" style="height:0px;opacity:0"><div class="text-primary max-w-2xl pr-11 pb-6 text-base leading-7 [&amp;_p+p]:mt-3 [&amp;_a]:text-primary/80 [&amp;_a]:underline [&amp;_a]:underline-offset-2 [&amp;_a:hover]:text-primary"><p>No. Subscriptions are only sold directly through X Subscription Corp.
